- **add**: A session task with no check needs --why saying why no check decides it (except --inspect). Add a task to the session run; its start snapshot is taken now. It says what --tests means for it — written (nitpick) or protected (a task with a check). --role subagent: the session's own sub-agent does it (declared hands, not self-performed); --scope: the files/dirs its hands own, so concurrent tasks split one tree by scope.
